FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n186\t    );\n187\t\n188\t    -- Blocked Issues Cache (Materialized view)\n189\t    -- Rebuilt on dependency or status changes.\n190\t   );\n280\t    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_worker_sessions_worker ON worker_sessions(worker_id);\n281\t    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_worker_sessions_model ON worker_sessions(model);\n282\t    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_worker_sessions_harness ON worker_sessions(harness);\n283\t\n284\t    -- Velocity Stats (bf-only table for performance-aware claim scoring)\n285\t    -- Aggregated statistics per (model, harness, issue_type) tuple.\n286\t    -- Updated on bead close to inform velocity-aware scoring.\n287\t    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS velocity_stats (\n288\t        model           TEXT NOT NULL,\n289\t        harness         TEXT NOT NULL,\n290\t        issue_type      TEXT NOT NULL,\n291\t        sample_count    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n292\t        p50_seconds Singleton table that prevents new claims during migration operations.\n302\t    -- Checked by bf claim at the start of each BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction.\n303\t    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS migration_lock (\n304\t        id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),\n305\t        locked_by   TEXT NOT NULL,\n306\t        locked_at   DATETIME NOT NULL,\n307\t        expires_at  DATETIME NOT NULL\n308\t    );\n309\t\";\n310\t\n311\t/// Split a SQL script into individual statements, respecting string literals,\n312\t/// quoted identifiers, and comments.\n313\t///\n314\t/// A naive `split(';')` breaks when SQL string literals contain semicolons\n315\t/// (e.g., `INSERT INTO t(v) VALUES('a;b')`). This function uses a small state\n316\t/// machine to track whether the current position is inside:\n317\t/// - A single-quoted string literal (`'...'`, with `''` as escape)\n318\t/// - A double-quoted identifier (`\"...\"`, with `\"\"` as escape)\n319\t/// - A line comment (`-- ...`)\n320\t/// - A block comment (`/* ... */`)\n321\t///\n322\t/// Only semicolons at the top level (outside all of the above) are treated as\n323\t/// statement terminators.\n324\tfn split_sql_statements(sql: &str) -> Vec<&str> {\n325\t    let bytes = sql.as_bytes();\n326\t    let len = bytes.len();\n327\t    let mut stmts = Vec::new();\n328\t    let mut start = 0; // byte offset where the current statement begins\n329\t    let mut i = 0;\n330\t\n331\t    // State flags — at most one is true at a time.\n332\t    let mut in_single_quote = false;\n333\t    let mut in_double_quote = false;\n334\t    let mut in_line_comment = false;\n335\t    let mut in_block_comment = false;\n336\t\n337\t    while i < len {\n338\t        let b = bytes[i];\n339\t\n340\t        // --- Line comment state ---\n341\t        if in_line_comment {\n342\t            if b == b'\\n' {\n343\t                in_line_comment = false;\n344\t            }\n345\t            i += 1;\n346\t            continue;\n347\t        }\n348\t\n349\t        // --- Block comment state ---\n350\t        if in_block_comment {\n351\t            if b == b'*' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'/' {\n352\t                in_block_comment = false;\n353\t                i += 2;\n354\t            } else {\n355\t                i += 1;\n356\t            }\n357\t            continue;\n358\t        }\n359\t\n360\t        // --- Single-quoted string state ---\n361\t        if in_single_quote {\n362\t            if b == b'\\'' {\n363\t                // '' is an escaped quote inside a string literal\n364\t                if i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'\\'' {\n365\t                    i += 2;\n366\t                } else {\n367\t                    in_single_quote = false;\n368\t                    i += 1;\n369\t                }\n370\t            } else {\n371\t                i += 1;\n372\t            }\n373\t            continue;\n374\t        }\n375\t\n376\t        // --- Double-quoted identifier state ---\n377\t        if in_double_quote {\n378\t            if b == b'\"' {\n379\t                if i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'\"' {\n380\t                    i += 2;\n381\t                } else {\n382\t                    in_double_quote = false;\n383\t                    i += 1;\n384\t                }\n385\t            } else {\n386\t                i += 1;\n387\t            }\n388\t            continue;\n389\t        }\n390\t\n391\t        // --- Top-level parsing ---\n392\t        if b == b'\\'' {\n393\t            in_single_quote = true;\n394\t            i += 1;\n395\t        } else if b == b'\"' {\n396\t            in_double_quote = true;\n397\t            i += 1;\n398\t        } else if b == b'-' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'-' {\n399\t            in_line_comment = true;\n400\t            i += 2;\n401\t        } else if b == b'/' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'*' {\n402\t            in_block_comment = true;\n403\t            i += 2;\n404\t        } else if b == b';' {\n405\t            // Statement terminator at top level.\n406\t            let stmt = &sql[start..i];\n407\t            if !stmt.trim().is_empty() {\n408\t                stmts.push(stmt.trim());\n409\t            }\n410\t            start = i + 1;\n411\t            i += 1;\n412\t        } else {\n413\t            i += 1;\n414\t        }\n415\t    }\n416\t\n417\t    // Trailing statement without a final semicolon.\n418\t    if start < len {\n419\t        let stmt = &sql[start..len];\n420\t        if !stmt.trim().is_empty() {\n421\t            stmts.push(stmt.trim());\n422\t        }\n423\t    }\n424\t\n425\t    stmts\n426\t}\n427\t\n428\t/// Execute multiple SQL statements separated by semicolons.\n429\tfn execute_batch(conn: &Connection, sql: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n430\t    conn.execute_batch(sql)?;\n431\t    Ok(())\n432\t}\n433\t\n434\tpub fn apply_schema(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n435\t    execute_batch(conn, SCHEMA_SQL)?;\n436\t    apply_migrations(conn)?;\n437\t    Ok(())\n438\t}\n439\t\n440\t/// Apply database migrations for schema changes.\n441\t/// This handles adding new columns to existing tables.\n442\tfn apply_migrations(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n443\t    // Migration 1: Update critical_path_cache schema if it has the old format\n444\t    // Old format: bead_id, float (REAL), updated_at\n445\t    // New format: bead_id, epic_id, es (INTEGER), ls (INTEGER), float (INTEGER), updated_at\n446\t    let has_es: bool = conn.query_row(\n447\t        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('critical_path_cache') WHERE name='es'\",\n448\t        [],\n449\t        |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0).map(|n| n > 0),\n450\t    ).unwrap_or(false);\n451\t\n452\t    if !has_es {\n453\t        // Table has old schema - recreate with new schema\n454\t        conn.execute_batch(\n455\t            \"BEGIN;\n456\t             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS critical_path_cache_new (\n457\t                 bead_id     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n458\t                 epic_id     TEXT,\n459\t                 es          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n460\t                 ls          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n461\t                 float       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n462\t                 updated_at  DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n463\t                 FOREIGN KEY (bead_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n464\t             );\n465\t             INSERT INTO critical_path_cache_new (bead_id, float, updated_at)\n466\t                 SELECT bead_id, CAST(float AS INTEGER), updated_at FROM critical_path_cache;\n467\t             DROP TABLE critical_path_cache;\n468\t             ALTER TABLE critical_path_cache_new RENAME TO critical_path_cache;\n469\t             COMMIT;\"\n470\t        )?;\n471\t    }\n472\t\n473\t    // Create indexes for critical_path_cache (idempotent)\n474\t    let _ = conn.execute_batch(\n475\t        \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_critical_path_cache_epic ON critical_path_cache(epic_id);\n476\t         CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_critical_path_cache_float ON critical_path_cache(float);\"\n477\t    );\n478\t\n479\t    // Migration 2: Add workspace_path to worker_sessions if missing\n480\t    // Older databases may not have this column; br never reads worker_sessions so this is safe.\n481\t    let has_workspace_path: bool = conn.query_row(\n482\t        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('worker_sessions') WHERE name='workspace_path'\",\n483\t        [],\n484\t        |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0).map(|n| n > 0),\n485\t    ).unwrap_or(false);\n486\t\n487\t    if !has_workspace_path {\n488\t        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE worker_sessions ADD COLUMN workspace_path TEXT\", [])?;\n489\t    }\n490\t\n491\t    // Migration 3: Add closed_at and duration_seconds to worker_sessions for velocity tracking\n492\t    let has_closed_at: bool = conn.query_row(\n493\t        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('worker_sessions') WHERE name='closed_at'\",\n494\t        [],\n495\t        |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0).map(|n| n > 0),\n496\t    ).unwrap_or(false);\n497\t\n498\t    if !has_closed_at {\n499\t        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE worker_sessions ADD COLUMN closed_at DATETIME\", [])?;\n500\t        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE worker_sessions ADD COLUMN duration_seconds INTEGER\", [])?;\n501\t    }\n502\t\n503\t    Ok(())\n504\t}\n505\t\n506\tpub fn ensure_wal_mode(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n507\t    // Use execute_batch so PRAGMAs that return rows don't cause errors\n508\t    conn.execute_batch(\n509\t        \"PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;\n510\t         PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;\n511\t         PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;\n512\t         PRAGMA cache_size = -8000;\n513\t         PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;\"\n514\t    )?;\n515\t    Ok(())\n516\t}\n517\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"74f424ae-6283-4255-bac2-5ba4acaafad8","uuid":"ab7c3dac-1d1b-49cd-8fef-e6bfb2969fff","timestamp":"2026-05-09T00:48:43.928Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/coding/bead-forge/src/storage/schema.rs","content":"//! Database schema definitions matching beads_rust (br) for interoperability.\n\nuse rusqlite::Connection;\n\n/// The complete SQL schema for the beads database.\n/// Schema matches beads_rust (br) for interoperability.\npub const SCHEMA_SQL: &str = r\"\n    -- Issues table\n    -- Note: TEXT fields use DEFAULT '' for bd (Go) compatibility.\n    -- bd's sql.Scan doesn't handle NULL well when scanning into string fields.\n    -- Closed-at invariant is enforced by the CHECK clause below.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS issues (\n        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        content_hash TEXT,\n        title TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(title) <= 500),\n        description TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n        design TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n        acceptance_criteria TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n        notes TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',\n        priority INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 2 CHECK(priority >= 0 AND priority <= 4),\n        issue_type TEXT NOT NULL due_at DATETIME,\n        defer_until DATETIME,\n        external_ref TEXT,\n        source_system TEXT DEFAULT '',\n        source_repo TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '.',\n        deleted_at DATETIME,\n        deleted_by TEXT DEFAULT '',\n        delete_reason TEXT DEFAULT '',\n        original_type TEXT DEFAULT '',\n        compaction_level INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n        compacted_at DATETIME,\n        compacted_at_commit TEXT,\n        original_size INTEGER,\n        sender TEXT DEFAULT '',\n        ephemeral INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        pinned INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        is_template INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        CHECK (\n            (status = 'closed' AND closed_at IS NOT NULL) OR\n            (status = 'tombstone') OR\n            (status NOT IN ('closed', 'tombstone') AND closed_at IS NULL)\n        )\n    );\n\n    -- Primary access patterns\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_status ON issues(status);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_priority ON issues(priority);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_issue_type ON issues(issue_type);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_assignee ON issues(assignee) WHERE assignee IS NOT NULL;\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_created_at ON issues(created_at);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_updated_at ON issues(updated_at);\n\n    -- Export/sync patterns\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_content_hash ON issues(content_hash);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_external_ref ON issues(external_ref) WHERE external_ref IS NOT NULL;\n    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_external_ref_unique ON issues(external_ref) WHERE external_ref IS NOT NULL;\n\n    -- Special states\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_ready\n        ON issues(status, priority, created_at)\n        WHERE status = 'open'\n        AND ephemeral = 0\n        AND pinned = 0\n        AND is_template = 0;\n\n    -- Common active list path: non-terminal issues sorted by priority/created_at\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_issues_list_active_order\n        ON issues(priority, created_at DESC)\n        WHERE status NOT IN ('closed', 'tombstone')\n        AND (is_template = 0 OR is_template IS NULL);\n\n    -- Dependencies\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dependencies (\n        issue_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n metadata TEXT DEFAULT '{}',\n        thread_id TEXT DEFAULT '',\n        PRIMARY KEY (issue_id, depends_on_id),\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n        -- Note: depends_on_id FK intentionally removed to allow external issue references\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dependencies_issue ON dependencies(issue_id);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT blocking lookups\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dependencies_blocking\n        ON dependencies(depends_on_id, issue_id)\n        WHERE (type = 'blocks' OR type = 'parent-child' OR type = 'conditional-blocks' OR type = 'waits-for');\n\n    -- Labels\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS labels (\n        issue_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n        label TEXT NOT NULL,\n        PRIMARY KEY (issue_id, label),\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_labels_label ON labels(label);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_labels_issue ON labels(issue_id);\n\n    -- Comments\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS comments (\n        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n        issue_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n        author TEXT NOT NULL,\n        text TEXT NOT NULL,\n        created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_comments_issue ON comments(issue_id);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_comments_created_at ON comments(created_at);\n\n    -- Events (Audit)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (\n        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n        issue_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n        event_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n        actor TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n        old_value TEXT,\n        new_value TEXT,\n        comment TEXT,\n        created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n    CREATE events(created_at);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_actor ON events(actor) WHERE actor != '';\n\n    -- Config (Runtime)\n    -- NOTE: Avoid PRIMARY KEY/UNIQUE constraints here because the current\n    -- storage engine does not reliably maintain unique autoindexes.\n    -- Application code enforces key replacement via DELETE + INSERT.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS config (\n        key TEXT NOT NULL,\n        value TEXT NOT NULL\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_config_key ON config(key);\n\n    -- Metadata\n    -- Same rationale as config: keep it as key-value with explicit index.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata (\n        key TEXT NOT NULL,\n        value TEXT NOT NULL\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_metadata_key ON metadata(key);\n\n    -- Dirty Issues (for export)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dirty_issues (\n        issue_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        marked_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dirty_issues_marked_at ON dirty_issues(marked_at);\n\n    -- Export Hashes (for incremental export)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS export_hashes (\n        issue_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        content_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n        exported_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n\n    -- Blocked Issues Cache (Materialized view)\n    -- Rebuilt on dependency or status changes.\n    -- `blocked_by` stores a JSON array of blocking issue IDs.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blocked_issues_cache (\n        issue_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        blocked_by TEXT NOT NULL,\n        blocked_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (issue_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_blocked_cache_blocked_at ON blocked_issues_cache(blocked_at);\n\n    -- Child Counters (for hierarchical IDs like bd-abc.1, bd-abc.2)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS child_counters (\n        parent_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        last_child INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        FOREIGN KEY (parent_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n\n    -- Recovery Sessions (for anomaly/recovery audit trail)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recovery_sessions (\n        correlation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        started_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n        completed_at TEXT,\n        final_outcome TEXT,\n        session_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n        trigger_reason TEXT,\n        workspace_path TEXT,\n        summary TEXT\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_recovery_sessions_started ON recovery_sessions(started_at);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_recovery_sessions_outcome ON recovery_sessions(final_outcome);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_recovery_sessions_type ON recovery_sessions(session_type);\n\n    -- Anomaly Audit Records (for anomaly/recovery event tracking)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anomaly_audit (\n        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n        correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n        timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,\n        event_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n        anomaly_type TEXT,\n        recovery_strategy TEXT,\n        outcome TEXT,\n        evidence TEXT,\n        metadata TEXT,\n        created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP),\n        FOREIGN KEY (correlation_id) REFERENCES recovery_sessions(correlation_id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_correlation ON anomaly_audit(correlation_id);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_timestamp ON anomaly_audit(timestamp);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_event_type ON anomaly_audit(event_type);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_anomaly_type ON anomaly_audit(anomaly_type);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anomaly_audit_outcome ON anomaly_audit(outcome);\n\n    -- Critical Path Cache (for impact-weighted claim scoring)\n    -- Computed from dependency graph; lower float = more critical (0 = on critical path)\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS critical_path_cache (\n        bead_id     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n        epic_id     TEXT REFERENCES issues(id),\n        es          INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- earliest start (hops from root)\n        ls          INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- latest start\n        float       INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- ls - es; 0 = critical path\n        updated_at  DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        FOREIGN KEY (bead_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n    );\n\n    -- Bead Annotations (bf-only table, never touched by br)\n    -- Stores arbitrary key-value metadata per bead.\n    -- IMPORTANT: This is a SEPARATE table (not a column on issues) because br's\n    -- issues_column_order_matches() check triggers rebuild_issues_table() when\n    -- the column count differs, which would silently destroy any extra column.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bead_annotations (\n        bead_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n        key     TEXT NOT NULL,\n        value   TEXT NOT NULL,\n        PRIMARY KEY (bead_id, key)\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bead_annotations_key_value\n        ON bead_annotations (key, value);\n\n    -- Worker Sessions (bf-only table for multi-workspace claiming)\n    -- Tracks worker metadata (model, harness, version) for each claim operation.\n    -- Used by velocity-aware scoring and audit trails.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS worker_sessions (\n        worker_id        TEXT NOT NULL,\n        model            TEXT,\n        harness          TEXT,\n        harness_version  TEXT,\n        claimed_at       DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n        bead_id          TEXT REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n        workspace_path   TEXT NOT NULL,\n        PRIMARY KEY (worker_id, claimed_at)\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_worker_sessions_worker ON worker_sessions(worker_id);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_worker_sessions_model ON worker_sessions(model);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_worker_sessions_harness ON worker_sessions(harness);\n\n    -- Velocity Stats (bf-only table for performance-aware claim scoring)\n    -- Aggregated statistics per (model, harness, issue_type) tuple.\n    -- Updated on bead close to inform velocity-aware scoring.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS velocity_stats (\n        model           TEXT NOT NULL,\n        harness         TEXT NOT NULL,\n        issue_type      TEXT NOT NULL,\n        sample_count    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n        p50_seconds     INTEGER,\n        p90_seconds     INTEGER,\n        avg_seconds     REAL,\n        last_updated    DATETIME,\n        PRIMARY KEY (model, harness, issue_type)\n    );\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_velocity_stats_last_updated ON velocity_stats(last_updated);\n\n    -- Migration Lock (bf-only table for coordinating workspace migrations)\n    -- Singleton table that prevents new claims during migration operations.\n    -- Checked by bf claim at the start of each BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS migration_lock (\n        id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),\n        locked_by   TEXT NOT NULL,\n        locked_at   DATETIME NOT NULL,\n        expires_at  DATETIME NOT NULL\n    );\n\";\n\n/// Split a SQL script into individual statements, respecting string literals,\n/// quoted identifiers, and comments.\n///\n/// A naive `split(';')` breaks when SQL string literals contain semicolons\n/// (e.g., `INSERT INTO t(v) VALUES('a;b')`). This function uses a small state\n/// machine to track whether the current position is inside:\n/// - A single-quoted string literal (`'...'`, with `''` as escape)\n/// - A double-quoted identifier (`\"...\"`, with `\"\"` as escape)\n/// - A line comment (`-- ...`)\n/// - A block comment (`/* ... */`)\n///\n/// Only semicolons at the top level (outside all of the above) are treated as\n/// statement terminators.\nfn split_sql_statements(sql: &str) -> Vec<&str> {\n    let bytes = sql.as_bytes();\n    let len = bytes.len();\n    let mut stmts = Vec::new();\n    let mut start = 0; // byte offset where the current statement begins\n    let mut i = 0;\n\n    // State flags — at most one is true at a time.\n    let mut in_single_quote = false;\n    let mut in_double_quote = false;\n    let mut in_line_comment = false;\n    let mut in_block_comment = false;\n\n    while i < len {\n        let b = bytes[i];\n\n        // --- Line comment state ---\n        if in_line_comment {\n            if b == b'\\n' {\n                in_line_comment = false;\n            }\n            i += 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // --- Block comment state ---\n        if in_block_comment {\n            if b == b'*' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'/' {\n                in_block_comment = false;\n                i += 2;\n            } else {\n                i += 1;\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // --- Single-quoted string state ---\n        if in_single_quote {\n            if b == b'\\'' {\n                // '' is an escaped quote inside a string literal\n                if i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'\\'' {\n                    i += 2;\n                } else {\n                    in_single_quote = false;\n                    i += 1;\n                }\n            } else {\n                i += 1;\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // --- Double-quoted identifier state ---\n        if in_double_quote {\n            if b == b'\"' {\n                if i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'\"' {\n                    i += 2;\n                } else {\n                    in_double_quote = false;\n                    i += 1;\n                }\n            } else {\n                i += 1;\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // --- Top-level parsing ---\n        if b == b'\\'' {\n            in_single_quote = true;\n            i += 1;\n        } else if b == b'\"' {\n            in_double_quote = true;\n            i += 1;\n        } else if b == b'-' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'-' {\n            in_line_comment = true;\n            i += 2;\n        } else if b == b'/' && i + 1 < len && bytes[i + 1] == b'*' {\n            in_block_comment = true;\n            i += 2;\n        } else if b == b';' {\n            // Statement terminator at top level.\n            let stmt = &sql[start..i];\n            if !stmt.trim().is_empty() {\n                stmts.push(stmt.trim());\n            }\n            start = i + 1;\n            i += 1;\n        } else {\n            i += 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Trailing statement without a final semicolon.\n    if start < len {\n        let stmt = &sql[start..len];\n        if !stmt.trim().is_empty() {\n            stmts.push(stmt.trim());\n        }\n    }\n\n    stmts\n}\n\n/// Execute multiple SQL statements separated by semicolons.\nfn execute_batch(conn: &Connection, sql: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    conn.execute_batch(sql)?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub fn apply_schema(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    execute_batch(conn, SCHEMA_SQL)?;\n    apply_migrations(conn)?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Apply database migrations for schema changes.\n/// This handles adding new columns to existing tables.\nfn apply_migrations(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    // Migration 1: Update critical_path_cache schema if it has the old format\n    // Old format: bead_id, float (REAL), updated_at\n    // New format: bead_id, epic_id, es (INTEGER), ls (INTEGER), float (INTEGER), updated_at\n    let has_es: bool = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('critical_path_cache') WHERE name='es'\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0).map(|n| n > 0),\n    ).unwrap_or(false);\n\n    if !has_es {\n        // Table has old schema - recreate with new schema\n        conn.execute_batch(\n            \"BEGIN;\n             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS critical_path_cache_new (\n                 bead_id     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n                 epic_id     TEXT,\n                 es          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n                 ls          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n                 float       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n                 updated_at  DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                 FOREIGN KEY (bead_id) REFERENCES issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n             );\n             INSERT INTO critical_path_cache_new (bead_id, float, updated_at)\n                 SELECT bead_id, CAST(float AS INTEGER), updated_at FROM critical_path_cache;\n             DROP TABLE critical_path_cache;\n             ALTER TABLE critical_path_cache_new RENAME TO critical_path_cache;\n             COMMIT;\"\n        )?;\n    }\n\n    // Create indexes for critical_path_cache (idempotent)\n    let _ = conn.execute_batch(\n        \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_critical_path_cache_epic ON critical_path_cache(epic_id);\n         CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_critical_path_cache_float ON critical_path_cache(float);\"\n    );\n\n    // Migration 2: Add workspace_path to worker_sessions if missing\n    // Older databases may not have this column; br never reads worker_sessions so this is safe.\n    let has_workspace_path: bool = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('worker_sessions') WHERE name='workspace_path'\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0).map(|n| n > 0),\n    ).unwrap_or(false);\n\n    if !has_workspace_path {\n        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE worker_sessions ADD COLUMN workspace_path TEXT\", [])?;\n    }\n\n    // Migration 3: Add closed_at and duration_seconds to worker_sessions for velocity tracking\n    let has_closed_at: bool = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('worker_sessions') WHERE name='closed_at'\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0).map(|n| n > 0),\n    ).unwrap_or(false);\n\n    if !has_closed_at {\n        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE worker_sessions ADD COLUMN closed_at DATETIME\", [])?;\n        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE worker_sessions ADD COLUMN duration_seconds INTEGER\", [])?;\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub fn ensure_wal_mode(conn: &Connection) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    // Use execute_batch so PRAGMAs that return rows don't cause errors\n    conn.execute_batch(\n        \"PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;\n         PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;\n         PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;\n         PRAGMA cache_size = -8000;\n         PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;\"\n    )?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n","numLines":517,"startLine":1,"totalLines":517}}}

      #[arg(long)]\n480\t        skip_verify: bool,\n481\t    },\n482\t}\n483\t\n484\t#[derive(Subcommand)]\n485\tpub enum DepCommands {\n486\t    /// Add a dependency\n487\t    Add {\n488\t },\n635\t}\n636\t\n637\tpub fn run_cli() -> Result<Cli> {\n638\t    Ok(Cli::try_parse()?)\n639\t}\n640\t\n641\tpub fn run(cli: Cli) -> Result<()> {\n642\t    let workspace = cli.workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(\".\"));\n643\t\n644\t    // For init command, we allow the .beads directory to not exist yet\n645\t    match &cli.command {\n646\t        Commands::Init { .. } => {\n647\t            let beads_dir = workspace.join(\".beads\");\n648\t            return match cli.command {\n649\t                Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n650\t                _ => unreachable!(),\n651\t            };\n652\t        }\n653\t        _ => {}\n654\t    }\n655\t\n656\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(&workspace)\n657\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace))?;\n658\t\n659\t    match cli.command {\n660\t        Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n661\t        Commands::Create { title, type_, priority, description, assignee, label } => {\n662\t            cmd_create(&beads_dir, title, type_, priority, description, assignee, label)\n663\t        }\n664\t        Commands::List { status, type_, assignee, priority, limit, all, format, json } => {\n665\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n666\t            cmd_list(&beads_dir, status, type_, assignee, priority, limit, all, &format)\n667\t        }\n668\t        Commands::Show { id, format, json } => {\n669\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n670\t            cmd_show(&beads_dir, &id, &format)\n671\t        }\n672\t        Commands::Update { id, title, status, priority, assignee } => {\n673\t            cmd_update(&beads_dir, &id, title, status, priority, assignee)\n674\t        }\n675\t        Commands::Close { id, reason } => cmd_close(&beads_dir, &id, &reason),\n676\t        Commands::Reopen { json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n680\t            cmd_ready(&beads_dir, limit, &format)\n681\t        }\n682\t        Commands::Claim { assignee, model, harness, harness_version, any, fallback, workspace_paths, dry_run, format, json } => {\n683\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n684\t            cmd_claim(&beads_dir, &assignee, model, harness, harness_version, any, fallback.as_deref(), &workspace_paths, dry_run, &format)\n685\t        }\n686\t        Commands::Sync { flush_only, import_only } => cmd_sync(&beads_dir, flush_only, import_only),\n687\t        Commands::Doctor { repair, reclaim_stale, ttl } => cmd_doctor(&beads_dir, repair, reclaim_stale, ttl),\n688\t        Commands::CommitCheck => cmd_commit_check(&beads_dir),\n689\t        Commands::Count { status } => cmd_count(&beads_dir, status),\n690\t        Commands::Batch { file, json, stdin } => cmd_batch(&beads_dir, file, json, stdin),\n691\t        Commands::Mitosis { id, children, reason, format } => cmd_mitosis(&beads_dir, &id, &children, &reason, &format),\n692\t        Commands::Dep(dep) => cmd_dep(&beads_dir, dep),\n693\t        Commands::Label(label) => cmd_label(&beads_dir, label),\n694\t        Commands::Comments(comments) => cmd_comments(&beads_dir, comments),\n695\t        Commands::Search { query, status, type_, assignee, label, priority_min, priority_max, limit, format } => {\n696\t            cmd_search(&beads_dir, query, status, type_, assignee, label, priority_min, priority_max, limit, &format)\n697\t        }\n698\t        Commands::Stats { by_type, by_priority, by_assignee, by_label, format } => {\n699\t            cmd_stats(&beads_dir, by_type, by_priority, by_assignee, by_label, &format)\n700\t        }\n701\t        Commands::Schema { target, format } => cmd_schema(&target, &format),\n702\t        Commands::Config(config) => cmd_config(&beads_dir, config),\n703\t        Commands::Velocity { model, harness, format } => cmd_velocity(&beads_dir, model, harness, &format),\n704\t        Commands::Labels { id, format } => cmd_labels(&beads_dir, &id, &format),\n705\t        Commands::Annotate(annotate) => cmd_annotate(&beads_dir, annotate),\n706\t        Commands::Log { id, limit, since, actor, status_changes, diff, format, json } => {\n707\t            let format = if Commands::Migrate { workspace, from_jsonl, seed_velocity, dry_run, skip_verify } => {\n713\t            cmd_migrate(&beads_dir, workspace, from_jsonl, seed_velocity, dry_run, skip_verify)\n714\t        }\n715\t    }\n716\t}\n717\t\n718\tfn cmd_init(beads_dir: &PathBuf, prefix: &str) -> Result<()> {\n719\t    std::fs::create_dir_all(beads_dir)?;\n720\t\n721\t    let config_path = beads_dir.join(\"config.yaml\");\n722\t    if !config_path.exists() {\n723\t        let config = format!(\n724\t            r#\"issue_prefixes: [{}]\n725\tdefault_priority: 2\n726\tdefault_type: task\n727\tclaim_ttl_minutes: 30\n728\t\"#,\n729\t            prefix\n730\t        );\n731\t        std::fs::write(&config_path, config)?;\n732\t    }\n733\t\n734\t    let metadata_path = beads_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n735\t    if !metadata_path.exists() {\n736\t        let metadata = r#\"{\"database\": \"beads.db\", \"jsonl_export\": \"issues.jsonl\"}\"#;\n737\t        std::fs::write(&metadata_path, metadata)?;\n738\t    }\n739\t\n740\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\");\n741\t    let _storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n742\t\n743\t    let gitignore_path = beads_dir.join(\".gitignore\");\n744\t    if !gitignore_path.exists() {\n745\t        std::fs::write(&gitignore_path, \"beads.db\\nbeads.db-shm\\nbeads.db-wal\\n\")?;\n746\t    }\n747\t\n748\t    println!(\"Initialized bead-forge workspace in {:?}\", beads_dir);\n749\t    Ok(())\n750\t}\n751\t\n752\tfn cmd_create(\n753\t    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n754\t    title: String,\n755\t    type_: String,\n756\t    priority: i32,\n757\t    description: Option<String>,\n758\t    assignee: Option<String>,\n759\t    labels: Vec<String>,\n760\t) -> Result<()> {\n761\t    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n762\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n763\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n764\t    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n765\t\n766\t    let count = storage.count_issues()?;\n767\t    let prefix = get_default_prefix(&config);\n768\t    let id = crate::id::generate_id(prefix, count);\n769\t\n770\t    let mut issue = Issue::new(id.clone(), title, \".\".to_string());\n771\t    issue.issue_type = IssueType::from_str(type_.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n772\t    issue.priority = Priority(priority);\n773\t    issue.description = description;\n774\t    issue.assignee = assignee;\n775\t    issue.labels = labels;\n776\t\n777\t    storage.create_issue(&issue)?;\n778\t\n779\t    println!(\"{}\", id);\n780\t    Ok(())\n781\t}\n782\t\n783\tfn cmd_list(\n784\t    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n785\t    status: Option<String>,\n786\t    type_: Option<String>,\n787\t    assignee: Option<String>,\n788\t    priority: Option<i32>,\n789\t    limit: Option<usize>,\n790\t    all: bool,\n791\t    format: &str,\n792\t) -> Result<()> {\n793\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n794\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n795\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n796\t\n797\t    let mut issues = if all {\n798\t        // Include archived beads\n799\t        list_all_with_archives(beads_dir)?\n800\t    } else {\n801\t        // Only from database\n802\t        let mut filter = IssueFilter::default();\n803\t        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n804\t            filter.status = Some(Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n805\t        }\n806\t        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n807\t            filter.issue_type = Some(IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n808\t        }\n809\t        filter.assignee = assignee.clone();\n810\t        filter.priority = priority;\n811\t        // --limit 0 means unlimited\n812\t        filter.limit = limit.and_then(|l| if l == 0 { None } else { Some(l) });\n813\t        storage.list_issues(&filter)?\n814\t    };\n815\t\n816\t    // Apply additional filters for --all mode (since we're not using DB filters)\n817\t    if all {\n818\t        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n819\t            let status_filter = Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n820\t            issues.retain(|i| i.status == status_filter);\n821\t        }\n822\t        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n823\t            let type_filter = IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n824\t            issues.retain(|i| i.issue_type == type_filter);\n825\t        }\n826\t        if let Some(ref assignee_val) = assignee {\n827\t            issues.retain(|i| i.assignee.as_deref() == Some(assignee_val));\n828\t        }\n829\t        if let Some(p) = priority {\n830\t            issues.retain(|i| i.priority.0 == p);\n831\t        }\n832\t        // Apply limit\n833\t        if let Some(l) = limit {\n834\t            if l != 0 {\n835\t                issues.truncate(l);\n836\t            }\n837\t        }\n838\t    }\n839\t\n840\t    let output_format = OutputFormat::from_str(format).unwrap_or(OutputFormat::Text);\n841\t    let formatter = get_formatter(output_format);\n842\t    print!(\"{}\", formatter.format_issues(&issues));\n843\t\n844\t    Ok(())\n845\t}\n846\t\n847\tfn cmd_show(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n848\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n849\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n850\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n851\t\n852\t    let issue = match storage.get_issue(id)? {\n853\t        Some(i) => i,\n854\t        None => {\n855\t            // Search archives\n856\t            find_bead_in_archives(beads_dir, id)?\n857\t                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"Bead not found: {}\", id))?\n858\t        }\n859\t    };\n860\t\n861\t    match format {\n862\t        \"json\" => {\n863\t            // Strip dependencies/comments before serializing: NEEDLE's BrDependency\n864\t            // format ({id, title, status, priority, dependency_type}) differs from\n865\t            // bead-forge's Dependency format ({issue_id, depends_on_id, type, ...}).\n866\t            // NEEDLE has #[serde(default)] on the deps field so empty is fine.\n867\t            let mut out = issue;\n868\t            out.dependencies = vec![];\n869\t            out.comments = vec![];\n870\t            // Wrap in array so NEEDLE's parse_single_bead (Vec<Bead> → first) works.\n871\t            println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string(&vec![out])?);\n872\t        }\n873\t        \"toon\" => {\n874\t            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n875\t            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n876\t            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n877\t            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n878\t            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n879\t            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n880\t                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n881\t            }\n882\t            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n883\t                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n884\t            }\n885\t            if !issue.labels.is_empty() {\n886\t                println!(\"Labels: {}\", issue.labels.join(\", \"));\n887\t            }\n888\t        }\n889\t        _ => {\n890\t            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n891\t            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n892\t            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n893\t            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n894\t            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n895\t            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n896\t                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n897\t            }\n898\t            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n899\t                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n900\t            }\n901\t            if !issue.labels.is_empty() {\n902\t                println!(\"Labels: {}\", issue.labels.join(\", \"));\n903\t            }\n904\t        }\n905\t    }\n906\t\n907\t    Ok(())\n908\t}\n909\t\n910\tfn cmd_update(\n911\t    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n912\t    id: &str,\n913\t    title: Option<String>,\n914\t    status: Option<String>,\n915\t    priority: Option<i32>,\n916\t    assignee: Option<String>,\n917\t) -> Result<()> {\n918\t    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n919\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n920\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n921\t    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n922\t\n923\t    let changes = IssueChanges {\n924\t        title,\n925\t        status: status.map(|s| Status::from_str(&s).ok()).flatten(),\n926\t        priority,\n927\t        assignee,\n928\t        ..Default::default()\n929\t    };\n930\t\n931\t    storage.update_issue(id, &changes)?;\n932\t    println!(\"Updated bead {}\", id);\n933\t    Ok(())\n934\t}\n935\t\n936\tfn cmd_close(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<()> {\n937\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n938\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n939\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n940\t\n941\t    storage.close_issue(id, reason, \"cli\")?;\n942\t    println!(\"Closed bead {}\", id);\n943\t    Ok(())\n944\t}\n945\t\n946\tfn cmd_reopen(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n947\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n948\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n949\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n950\t\n951\t    let changes = IssueChanges {\n952\t        status: Some(Status::Open),\n953\t        ..Default::default()\n954\t    };\n955\t\n956\t    storage.update_issue(id, &changes)?;\n957\t    println!(\"Reopened bead {}\", id);\n958\t    Ok(())\n959\t}\n960\t\n961\tfn cmd_delete(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n962\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n963\t    let db_path =    return Ok(());\n1415\t    }\n1416\t\n1417\t    // Status indicators: ●=open, ◐=in_progress, ○=closed/blocked/deferred, ⊘=tombstone\n1418\t    let status_symbol = |status: &str| -> char {\n1419\t        match status {\n1420\t            \"open\" => '●',\n1421\t            \"in_progress\" => '◐',\n1422\t            \"closed\" => '○',\n1423\t            \"blocked\" => '◌',\n1424\t            \"deferred\" => '○',\n1425\t            \"tombstone\" => '⊘',\n1426\t            _ => '○',\n1427\t        }\n1428\t    };\n1429\t\n1430\t    for (i, node) in nodes.iter().enumerate() {\n1431\t {\n1436\t            for d in 0..node.depth as usize {\n1437\t                if d < node.depth as usize - 1 {\n1438\t                    prefix.push_str(\"│   \");\n1439\t                } else {\n1440\t                    // Check if this is the last node at this depth\n1441\t                    let is_last = nodes.iter().skip(i + 1)\n1442\t                        .all(|n| n.depth < node.depth || (n.depth == node.depth && n.id > node.id));\n1443\t                    if is_last {\n1444\t                        prefix.push_str(\"└── \");\n1445\t                    } else {\n1446\t                        prefix.push_str(\"├── \");\n1447\t                    }\n1448\t                }\n1449\t            }\n1450\t        }\n1451\t\n1452\t        // Truncate title if too long\n1453\t        let title = if node.title.len() > 60 {\n1454\t            format!(\"{}...\", &node.title[..57])\n1455\t        } else {\n1456\t            node.title.clone()\n1457\t        };\n1458\t\n1459\t        let cycle_mark = if is_cycle { \" [CYCLE]\" } else { \"\" };\n1460\t        let dep_type_str = node.dep_type.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"blocks\");\n1461\t\n1462\t node.priority,\n1468\t            dep_type_str,\n1469\t            cycle_mark\n1470\t        );\n1471\t    }\n1472\t\n1473\t    Ok(())\n1474\t}\n1475\t\n1476\tfn cmd_dep(beads_dir: &PathBuf, dep: Subcommand};\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\nuse std::str::FromStr;\n\n#[derive(Parser)]\n#[command(name = \"bf\")]\n#[command(about = \"bead-forge - Drop-in replacement for beads_rust (br)\", long_about = None)]\npub struct Cli {\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    pub command: Commands,\n\n    /// Workspace directory (defaults to current directory's .beads/)\n    #[arg(short, long, global = true)]\n    pub workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum Commands {\n    /// Create a new bead\n    Create {\n        /// Bead title\n        #[arg(long)]\n        title: String,\n\n        /// Bead type\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"task\")]\n        type_: String,\n\n        /// Priority (0=Critical, 4=Backlog)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"2\")]\n        priority: i32,\n\n        /// Description\n        #[arg(long)]\n        description: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Assignee\n        #[arg(long)]\n        assignee: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Labels\n        #[arg(long)]\n        label: Vec<String>,\n    },\n\n    /// List beads\n    List {\n        /// Filter by status\n        #[arg(long)]\n        status: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by type\n        #[arg(long)]\n        type_: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by assignee\n        #[arg(long)]\n        assignee: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by priority\n        #[arg(long)]\n        priority: Option<i32>,\n\n        /// Limit results (0 = unlimited)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        limit: Option<usize>,\n\n        /// Include archived beads from archive files\n        #[arg(long)]\n        all: bool,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Show bead details\n    Show {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Update a bead\n    Update {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// New title\n        #[arg(long)]\n        title: Option<String>,\n\n        /// New status\n        #[arg(long)]\n        status: Option<String>,\n\n        /// New priority\n        #[arg(long)]\n        priority: Option<i32>,\n\n        /// New assignee\n        #[arg(long)]\n        assignee: Option<String>,\n    },\n\n    /// Close a bead\n    Close {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Close reason\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"Completed\")]\n        reason: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Reopen a bead\n    Reopen {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Delete a bead\n    Delete {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Show ready (unblocked) beads\n    Ready {\n        /// Limit results (0 = unlimited)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"10\")]\n        limit: usize,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Claim a bead (atomic)\n    Claim {\n        /// Assignee (worker ID)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        assignee: String,\n\n        /// Model\n        #[arg(long)]\n        model: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Harness\n        #[arg(long)]\n        harness: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Harness version\n        #[arg(long)]\n        harness_version: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Claim from any workspace (searches all .beads/ directories)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        any: bool,\n\n        /// Fallback mode: try current workspace first, fall back to any if no beads available\n        #[arg(long, value_name = \"MODE\")]\n        fallback: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Workspace paths to search (only used with --any)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        workspace_paths: Vec<PathBuf>,\n\n        /// Dry run (show what would be claimed without claiming)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        dry_run: bool,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Initialize a new workspace\n    Init {\n        /// Issue prefix\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"bf\")]\n        prefix: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Sync (flush to JSONL or import from JSONL)\n    Sync {\n        /// Flush only (SQLite -> JSONL)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        flush_only: bool,\n\n        /// Import only (JSONL -> SQLite)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        import_only: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Doctor - check and repair\n    Doctor {\n        /// Repair database\n        #[arg(long)]\n        repair: bool,\n\n        /// Reclaim stale in_progress beads (reset to open without claiming)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        reclaim_stale: bool,\n\n        /// TTL in minutes for stale bead detection (overrides config claim_ttl_minutes)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        ttl: Option<i64>,\n    },\n\n    /// Commit check - scan staged .beads/ changes for secrets (git pre-commit hook)\n    CommitCheck,\n\n    /// Count beads\n    Count {\n        /// Filter by status\n        #[arg(long)]\n        status: Option<String>,\n    },\n\n    /// Batch operations (atomic)\n    Batch {\n        /// JSON file containing operations\n        #[arg(long)]\n        file: Option<PathBuf>,\n\n        /// JSON string containing operations\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Read from stdin\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"false\")]\n        stdin: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Mitosis: split a bead into children atomically\n    Mitosis {\n        /// Parent bead ID to split\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Child bead definitions (JSON array of {title, type, priority})\n        #[arg(long)]\n        children: String,\n\n        /// Close reason for parent bead\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"Split into children\")]\n        reason: String,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Manage dependencies\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Dep(DepCommands),\n\n    /// Manage labels\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Label(LabelCommands),\n\n    /// List labels for a specific issue (direct SELECT, efficient)\n    Labels {\n        /// Bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json)\n        #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Manage comments\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Comments(CommentsCommands),\n\n    /// Search beads\n    Search {\n        /// Search query\n        query: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by status\n        #[arg(short, long)]\n        status: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by type\n        #[arg(short, long)]\n        type_: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by assignee\n        #[arg(long)]\n        assignee: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by label\n        #[arg(short, long)]\n        label: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by minimum priority\n        #[arg(long, value_name = \"MIN\")]\n        priority_min: Option<i32>,\n\n        /// Filter by maximum priority\n        #[arg(long, value_name = \"MAX\")]\n        priority_max: Option<i32>,\n\n        /// Limit results\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"50\")]\n        limit: usize,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Show statistics\n    Stats {\n        /// Show breakdown by type\n        #[arg(long)]\n        by_type: bool,\n\n        /// Show breakdown by priority\n        #[arg(long)]\n        by_priority: bool,\n\n        /// Show breakdown by assignee\n        #[arg(long)]\n        by_assignee: bool,\n\n        /// Show breakdown by label\n        #[arg(long)]\n        by_label: bool,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Emit JSON Schema\n    Schema {\n        /// Schema target\n        #[arg(default_value = \"all\")]\n        target: String,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"json\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Configuration management\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Config(ConfigCommands),\n\n    /// Show velocity stats (bead-forge specific)\n    Velocity {\n        /// Model\n        #[arg(long)]\n        model: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Harness\n        #[arg(long)]\n        harness: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Manage annotations\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    Annotate(AnnotateCommands),\n\n    /// Show event log for a bead\n    Log {\n        /// Bead ID (omit to show all events)\n        id: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Limit number of entries\n        #[arg(long)]\n        limit: Option<usize>,\n\n        /// Show events since this date (RFC3339 format, e.g., 2026-05-01T00:00:00Z)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        since: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Filter by actor (worker name)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        actor: Option<String>,\n\n        /// Show only status change events\n        #[arg(long)]\n        status_changes: bool,\n\n        /// Show field-level diff between old and new values\n        #[arg(long)]\n        diff: bool,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n\n        /// Output JSON (alias for --format json)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        json: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Show critical path (longest chain of blocking dependencies)\n    CriticalPath {\n        /// Root bead ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Maximum depth\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"20\")]\n        max_depth: usize,\n\n        /// Output format (text, json, toon)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"text\")]\n        format: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Rotate (archive) closed beads older than threshold\n    Rotate {\n        /// Days threshold (archive beads closed this many days ago)\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"30\")]\n        days: u64,\n\n        /// Dry run (show what would be rotated)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        dry_run: bool,\n    },\n\n    /// Migrate workspace from br to bf\n    Migrate {\n        /// Workspace path to migrate (defaults to current directory)\n        #[arg(short, long)]\n        workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n\n        /// Reimport from JSONL (for corrupted/missing databases)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        from_jsonl: bool,\n\n        /// Seed velocity stats from reconstructed events\n        #[arg(long)]\n        seed_velocity: bool,\n\n        /// Dry run (show what would be done without making changes)\n        #[arg(long)]\n        dry_run: bool,\n\n        /// Skip verification steps\n        #[arg(long)]\n        skip_verify: bool,\n    },\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum DepCommands {\n    /// Add a dependency\n    Add {\n        /// Issue ID (the one that will depend on something)\n        issue: String,\n\n        /// Target issue ID (the one being depended on)\n        depends_on: String,\n\n        /// Dependency type\n        #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"blocks\")]\n        type_: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Remove a dependency\n    Remove {\n        /// Issue ID\n        issue: String,\n\n        /// Target issue ID to remove dependency to\n        depends_on: String,\n    },\n\n    /// List dependencies of an issue\n    List {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Show dependency tree rooted at issue\n    Tree {\n        /// Issue ID (root of tree)\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Tree direction (down, up, both)\n        #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"down\")]\n        direction: String,\n\n        /// Maximum depth\n        #[arg(long, default_value = \"10\")]\n        max_depth: usize,\n    },\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum LabelCommands {\n    /// Add label(s) to an issue\n    Add {\n        /// Label(s) to add (multiple labels supported)\n        #[arg(short, long, required = true, num_args = 1..)]\n        label: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Remove label(s) from an issue\n    Remove {\n        /// Label(s) to remove (multiple labels supported)\n        #[arg(short, long, required = true, num_args = 1..)]\n        label: Vec<String>,\n\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// List labels for an issue or all unique labels\n    List {\n        /// Issue ID (optional - if omitted, lists all unique labels)\n        id: Option<String>,\n    },\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum CommentsCommands {\n    /// Add a comment\n    Add {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Comment text\n        #[arg(required = true, num_args = 1..)]\n        text: Vec<String>,\n    },\n\n    /// List comments for an issue\n    List {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum ConfigCommands {\n    /// List all config values\n    List,\n\n    /// Get a specific config value\n    Get {\n        /// Config key\n        key: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Show config file path\n    Path,\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\npub enum AnnotateCommands {\n    /// Set an annotation\n    Set {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Annotation key\n        key: String,\n\n        /// Annotation value\n        value: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Get an annotation\n    Get {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Annotation key\n        key: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Remove an annotation\n    Remove {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n\n        /// Annotation key\n        key: String,\n    },\n\n    /// List all annotations for an issue\n    List {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n\n    /// Clear all annotations for an issue\n    Clear {\n        /// Issue ID\n        id: String,\n    },\n}\n\npub fn run_cli() -> Result<Cli> {\n    Ok(Cli::try_parse()?)\n}\n\npub fn run(cli: Cli) -> Result<()> {\n    let workspace = cli.workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(\".\"));\n\n    // For init command, we allow the .beads directory to not exist yet\n    match &cli.command {\n        Commands::Init { .. } => {\n            let beads_dir = workspace.join(\".beads\");\n            return match cli.command {\n                Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n                _ => unreachable!(),\n            };\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(&workspace)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace))?;\n\n    match cli.command {\n        Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n        Commands::Create { title, type_, priority, description, assignee, label } => {\n            cmd_create(&beads_dir, title, type_, priority, description, assignee, label)\n        }\n        Commands::List { status, type_, assignee, priority, limit, all, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_list(&beads_dir, status, type_, assignee, priority, limit, all, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Show { id, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_show(&beads_dir, &id, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Update { id, title, status, priority, assignee } => {\n            cmd_update(&beads_dir, &id, title, status, priority, assignee)\n        }\n        Commands::Close { id, reason } => cmd_close(&beads_dir, &id, &reason),\n        Commands::Reopen { id } => cmd_reopen(&beads_dir, &id),\n        Commands::Delete { id } => cmd_delete(&beads_dir, &id),\n        Commands::Ready { limit, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_ready(&beads_dir, limit, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Claim { assignee, model, harness, harness_version, any, fallback, workspace_paths, dry_run, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_claim(&beads_dir, &assignee, model, harness, harness_version, any, fallback.as_deref(), &workspace_paths, dry_run, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Sync { flush_only, import_only } => cmd_sync(&beads_dir, flush_only, import_only),\n        Commands::Doctor { repair, reclaim_stale, ttl } => cmd_doctor(&beads_dir, repair, reclaim_stale, ttl),\n        Commands::CommitCheck => cmd_commit_check(&beads_dir),\n        Commands::Count { status } => cmd_count(&beads_dir, status),\n        Commands::Batch { file, json, stdin } => cmd_batch(&beads_dir, file, json, stdin),\n        Commands::Mitosis { id, children, reason, format } => cmd_mitosis(&beads_dir, &id, &children, &reason, &format),\n        Commands::Dep(dep) => cmd_dep(&beads_dir, dep),\n        Commands::Label(label) => cmd_label(&beads_dir, label),\n        Commands::Comments(comments) => cmd_comments(&beads_dir, comments),\n        Commands::Search { query, status, type_, assignee, label, priority_min, priority_max, limit, format } => {\n            cmd_search(&beads_dir, query, status, type_, assignee, label, priority_min, priority_max, limit, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Stats { by_type, by_priority, by_assignee, by_label, format } => {\n            cmd_stats(&beads_dir, by_type, by_priority, by_assignee, by_label, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Schema { target, format } => cmd_schema(&target, &format),\n        Commands::Config(config) => cmd_config(&beads_dir, config),\n        Commands::Velocity { model, harness, format } => cmd_velocity(&beads_dir, model, harness, &format),\n        Commands::Labels { id, format } => cmd_labels(&beads_dir, &id, &format),\n        Commands::Annotate(annotate) => cmd_annotate(&beads_dir, annotate),\n        Commands::Log { id, limit, since, actor, status_changes, diff, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_log(&beads_dir, id, limit, since, actor, status_changes, diff, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::CriticalPath { id, max_depth, format } => cmd_critical_path(&beads_dir, &id, max_depth, &format),\n        Commands::Rotate { days, dry_run } => cmd_rotate(&beads_dir, days, dry_run),\n        Commands::Migrate { workspace, from_jsonl, seed_velocity, dry_run, skip_verify } => {\n            cmd_migrate(&beads_dir, workspace, from_jsonl, seed_velocity, dry_run, skip_verify)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn cmd_init(beads_dir: &PathBuf, prefix: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    std::fs::create_dir_all(beads_dir)?;\n\n    let config_path = beads_dir.join(\"config.yaml\");\n    if !config_path.exists() {\n        let config = format!(\n            r#\"issue_prefixes: [{}]\ndefault_priority: 2\ndefault_type: task\nclaim_ttl_minutes: 30\n\"#,\n            prefix\n        );\n        std::fs::write(&config_path, config)?;\n    }\n\n    let metadata_path = beads_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n    if !metadata_path.exists() {\n        let metadata = r#\"{\"database\": \"beads.db\", \"jsonl_export\": \"issues.jsonl\"}\"#;\n        std::fs::write(&metadata_path, metadata)?;\n    }\n\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\");\n    let _storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let gitignore_path = beads_dir.join(\".gitignore\");\n    if !gitignore_path.exists() {\n        std::fs::write(&gitignore_path, \"beads.db\\nbeads.db-shm\\nbeads.db-wal\\n\")?;\n    }\n\n    println!(\"Initialized bead-forge workspace in {:?}\", beads_dir);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_create(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    title: String,\n    type_: String,\n    priority: i32,\n    description: Option<String>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    labels: Vec<String>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n\n    let count = storage.count_issues()?;\n    let prefix = get_default_prefix(&config);\n    let id = crate::id::generate_id(prefix, count);\n\n    let mut issue = Issue::new(id.clone(), title, \".\".to_string());\n    issue.issue_type = IssueType::from_str(type_.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n    issue.priority = Priority(priority);\n    issue.description = description;\n    issue.assignee = assignee;\n    issue.labels = labels;\n\n    storage.create_issue(&issue)?;\n\n    println!(\"{}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_list(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    status: Option<String>,\n    type_: Option<String>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    priority: Option<i32>,\n    limit: Option<usize>,\n    all: bool,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let mut issues = if all {\n        // Include archived beads\n        list_all_with_archives(beads_dir)?\n    } else {\n        // Only from database\n        let mut filter = IssueFilter::default();\n        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n            filter.status = Some(Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n            filter.issue_type = Some(IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n        }\n        filter.assignee = assignee.clone();\n        filter.priority = priority;\n        // --limit 0 means unlimited\n        filter.limit = limit.and_then(|l| if l == 0 { None } else { Some(l) });\n        storage.list_issues(&filter)?\n    };\n\n    // Apply additional filters for --all mode (since we're not using DB filters)\n    if all {\n        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n            let status_filter = Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n            issues.retain(|i| i.status == status_filter);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n            let type_filter = IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n            issues.retain(|i| i.issue_type == type_filter);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref assignee_val) = assignee {\n            issues.retain(|i| i.assignee.as_deref() == Some(assignee_val));\n        }\n        if let Some(p) = priority {\n            issues.retain(|i| i.priority.0 == p);\n        }\n        // Apply limit\n        if let Some(l) = limit {\n            if l != 0 {\n                issues.truncate(l);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let output_format = OutputFormat::from_str(format).unwrap_or(OutputFormat::Text);\n    let formatter = get_formatter(output_format);\n    print!(\"{}\", formatter.format_issues(&issues));\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_show(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let issue = match storage.get_issue(id)? {\n        Some(i) => i,\n        None => {\n            // Search archives\n            find_bead_in_archives(beads_dir, id)?\n                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"Bead not found: {}\", id))?\n        }\n    };\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            // Strip dependencies/comments before serializing: NEEDLE's BrDependency\n            // format ({id, title, status, priority, dependency_type}) differs from\n            // bead-forge's Dependency format ({issue_id, depends_on_id, type, ...}).\n            // NEEDLE has #[serde(default)] on the deps field so empty is fine.\n            let mut out = issue;\n            out.dependencies = vec![];\n            out.comments = vec![];\n            // Wrap in array so NEEDLE's parse_single_bead (Vec<Bead> → first) works.\n            println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string(&vec![out])?);\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n            }\n            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n            }\n            if !issue.labels.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"Labels: {}\", issue.labels.join(\", \"));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n            }\n            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n            }\n            if !issue.labels.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"Labels: {}\", issue.labels.join(\", \"));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_update(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    id: &str,\n    title: Option<String>,\n    status: Option<String>,\n    priority: Option<i32>,\n    assignee:
